# Agents · S · part 8

972 entries, screener-llm to sd. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **screener-llm**: 공고를 기준으로 이력서를 요약하고 채점하는 LLM 1차 스크리너의 관점으로 판정 JSON을 낸다. screen 스킬이 심사 시뮬레이션 때 호출한다. ([Power-xc/career-agent-kit](https://github.com/Power-xc/career-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/screener-llm.md) · ★1)
- **screener-recruiter**: 바쁜 리크루터가 이력서 첫 화면과 포트폴리오 첫 2쪽만 보고 다음 단계로 넘길지 정하는 관점의 판정 JSON을 낸다. screen 스킬이 심사 시뮬레이션 때 호출한다. ([Power-xc/career-agent-kit](https://github.com/Power-xc/career-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/screener-recruiter.md) · ★1)
- **screener_appraiser**: **Role.** Screen the candidate set to a final included set and rate each included source for quality. Consistency is the point — apply the same criteria to every source, including any user-supplied ones. ([PangenomeAI/academic-skills-food-nutrition](https://github.com/PangenomeAI/academic-skills-food-nutrition/blob/HEAD/food-research/agents/screener_appraiser.md) · ★43)

- **screening-agent**: Reviews AML/PEP screening work - watchlist matching, hit reason codes, append-only screening results, PII-safe logging. - **screening-agent**: Screens one company end to end and writes a Screening report with triage, knock-outs, founder, market and competitor research, a rubric scorecard and a price verdict. With a SkaFld VC platform connected it also reads and saves the deal there. ([SkafldStudio-Apps/SkaFld-VC-Plugin](https://github.com/SkafldStudio-Apps/SkaFld-VC-Plugin/blob/HEAD/skafld-vc/agents/screening-agent.md))

([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/blob/HEAD/plugins/moai-recruiter/agents/screening-auditor.md) · ★317)
- **screening-disposition**: The Screening Disposition agent (`core/agents/screening_disposition/`) turns one screening hit into a proposed `screening_disposition` an analyst can accept or override in under two minutes: per-identifier comparisons with cited evidence, a written rationale, a proposed outcome and a confidence band. **It never closes a hit, in any configuration.** ([mishrasanjeev/agentic-org](https://github.com/mishrasanjeev/agentic-org/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/screening-disposition.md) · ★15)

- **scribe**: Creates individual git commits as part of a commit sequence rewrite, with the ability to detect when commits are too large and ask the user how to split them ([dherman/claude-plugins](https://github.com/dherman/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/historian/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Sincronização mecânica de documentação depois que o trabalho já foi implementado e verificado — documento de estado, referência de API, guia de teste, marcação de specs como concluídas. Não decide o que foi feito, só registra o que já está pronto e comprovado. Substitua &lt;PROJETO&gt; e os caminhos pelos do seu repositório. ([DiegoAmorimDev/bks-multiagent-skill](https://github.com/DiegoAmorimDev/bks-multiagent-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/templates/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: The writing voice — turns rough material into clear, tight prose, and fine-combs existing text without flattening it. Drafts notes, technical docs, JIRA tickets, PR descriptions, commit messages, release notes, and messages; or wordsmiths a draft you already have. Writes in a concise, direct, semi-casual voice that uses apt analogies and the occasional dry bit of levity. Can draft from scratch OR tighten an existing draft. Trigger on intent — "write this up," "draft a PR description / ticket / note," "tighten this," "wordsmith this," "make this read better," "summarize this." ([dpep/claude](https://github.com/dpep/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/personas/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: PROACTIVELY DELEGATE documentation tasks to this agent. - **scribe**: Use for documents: drafting PRDs and specs, the client's org-context document (`.aplabs/context/org.md`, drafted from the Onboarder's findings and kept current on regular builds), the build ledger (a row per build, `DEP` rows for anything deferred or blocked), and proposed canon or doc updates when a build changes something a doc describes.
